political_actions = {
	GER_Flag_Picker = {
		icon = GFX_decision_ger_flag
		allowed = {
			original_tag = GER
		}
		visible = {
			is_ai = no
			OR = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = GER_EU
					has_country_flag = GER_can_form_impfed
					has_cosmetic_tag = GER_German_Greater
					has_cosmetic_tag = GER_greater
					has_cosmetic_tag = GER_greater_alt
				}
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		fire_only_once = no
		days_re_enable = 1
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 0
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision GER_Flag_Picker"
			country_event = gerdom.211
		}
	}
	GER_Force_Russian_Surrender = {
		icon = GFX_decision_rus_flag
		allowed = { tag = GER }
		available = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					DKB = {
						is_in_faction_with = GER
						has_completed_focus = DKB_the_foreigners_coup
					}
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				has_dictatorship_government = no
				surrender_progress > 0.5
			}
			surrender_progress < 0.3
			RUS_at_least_two_of_three_are_captured = yes
			OR = {
				RUS = { surrender_progress > 0.35 }
				AND = {
					OR = {
						202 = { is_controlled_by_ROOT_or_ally = yes }
						RUS = { NOT = { controls_state = 202 } }
					}
					OR = {
						206 = { is_controlled_by_ROOT_or_ally = yes }
						RUS = { NOT = { controls_state = 206 } }
					}
					OR = {
						96 = { is_controlled_by_ROOT_or_ally = yes }
						RUS = { NOT = { controls_state = 96 } }
					}		
					OR = {
						1091 = { is_controlled_by_ROOT_or_ally = yes }
						RUS = { NOT = { controls_state = 1091 } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
			NOT = { 
				has_global_flag = vilnius_accords_active
				RUS = { 
					tag = event_target:KR_internationale_leader 
					is_in_faction_with = INT
					is_in_faction_with = FRA
					is_subject = yes
				}
			}
		}
		targets = { RUS }
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				has_war_with = ROOT
				is_subject = no
				has_capitulated = no
				OR = {
					is_faction_leader = yes
					is_in_faction = no
				}
				NOT = { 
					tag = event_target:KR_internationale_leader 
					is_in_faction_with = INT
					is_in_faction_with = FRA
				}
			}
		}
		cost = freedec?0
		days_remove = 45
		
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1000
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					any_allied_country = {
						219 = { is_claim_or_core_of_PREV = yes }
					}
					any_other_country = {
						is_ai = no
						is_in_faction_with = GER
						has_war_with = RUS
					}
					has_game_rule = { rule = RUS_vilnius_accords option = RUS_VILNIUS_ACCORDS_DISABLED }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 2000
				has_game_rule = { rule = RUS_vilnius_accords option = RUS_VILNIUS_ACCORDS_GUARANTEED }
			}
		}
		
		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision complete GER_Force_Russian_Surrender"
			FROM = { country_event = rusfor.1528 }
		}
	}
	GER_appeal_to_old_friends = {
		icon = GFX_decision_dec_GER_central_power_knights

		allowed = {
			original_tag = GER
		}

		available = {
			FROM = { controls_all_owned_states = yes }
		}

		visible = {
			surrender_progress > 0.35
			is_subject = no
			has_socialist_government = no
			NOT = {
				is_in_faction_with = FROM
				has_war_with = FROM
			}
			any_enemy_country = {
				OR = {
					tag = FRA
					tag = ENG
					tag = RUS
				}
				NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
			}
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = GER_appealed_old_friends }
				is_subject = no
				has_socialist_government = no
				OR = {
					is_in_faction = no
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}

		target_root_trigger = {
			surrender_progress > 0.35
		}

		targets = { AUS TUR BUL OTT }

		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = GER_appealed_old_friends }
				is_subject = no
				has_socialist_government = no
				OR = {
					is_in_faction = no
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = no

		cost = 0

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 100
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision GER_appeal_to_old_friends target: [From.GetName]"
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { tag = AUS } }
				AUS = {
					set_country_flag = GER_appealed_old_friends
					country_event = {
						id = gerfor.51 days = 1
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { FROM = { tag = TUR } }
				TUR = {
					set_country_flag = GER_appealed_old_friends
					country_event = {
						id = gerfor.52 days = 1
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { FROM = { tag = BUL } }
				BUL = {
					set_country_flag = GER_appealed_old_friends
					country_event = {
						id = gerfor.53 days = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	GER_send_support_to_proxy = {
		icon = GFX_decision_support_equipment

		allowed = { original_tag = GER }

		available = {

			}

		}

				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
			}
		}

			has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > 1999 }
		}

		days_remove = 25

		target_root_trigger = {
			has_completed_focus = GER_Obligations_of_Empire
		}

		targets = { WRA COR TEX SPR }

		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				has_war = yes
				is_subject = no
				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
				OR = {
					has_idea = GER_German_Support1_idea
					has_idea = GER_German_Support2_idea
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { # This is to prevent the AI from spamming it which could annoy a player.
				factor = 0
				has_country_flag = GER_Sent_Support
			}
		}
		
		complete_effect = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment_1
				amount = -2000
			}
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision GER_send_support_to_proxy target: [From.GetName]"
			FROM = { country_event = gerfor.264 }
		}
	}

	GER_join_kaiser_wilhelm_society = {

			}
		}

		visible = {

				is_in_tech_sharing_group = german_research

			}
			FROM = {
				exists = yes

			}
		}

		targets = { GER }

		}

					limit = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
					remove_claim_by = ROOT
				}
				add_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
	}

GER_halifax_category = {

	GER_Halifax_Alsace_Lorraine = {
		icon = GFX_decision_dec_GER_halifax_al
		visible = {
			OR = {
				is_ai = no
				AND = {
					is_ai = yes
					CAN = { is_ai = yes }
					NFA = { is_ai = yes }
				}
			}
			OR = {
				original_tag = GER
				original_tag = CAN
				original_tag = NFA
			}
			NOT = { has_global_flag = GER_Halifax_Alsace_Lorraine_Clicked }
		}
		available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = GER_Halifax_Negotiating }
		}
		
		fire_only_once = yes
		
		cost = 10

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1000
		}
		
		complete_effect = {
			set_global_flag = GER_Halifax_Negotiating
			set_global_flag = GER_Halifax_Alsace_Lorraine_Clicked
			if = {
				limit = {
					tag = GER
					NFA = { is_in_faction_with = ENT }
				}
				NFA = {
					country_event = { id = entente.30701 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						tag = NFA
						tag = CAN
					}
					NFA = { is_in_faction_with = ENT }
				}
				GER = {
					country_event = { id = entente.30701 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { NFA = { is_in_faction_with = ENT } }
				}
				country_event = entente.30716
			}
		}
	}

GER_desperate_struggle_category = {

	GER_increase_struggle_level2 = {
		icon = GFX_decision_dec_GER_struggle
		
		allowed = { original_tag = GER }
		
		available = {
			OR = {
				surrender_progress > 0.30
				AND = {
					NOT = { controls_state = 5 }
					NOT = { controls_state = 776 }
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
			has_defensive_war = yes
			has_idea = GER_Desperate_Struggle1_idea
		}
		
		cost = 50
		days_remove = 30
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 300
		}
		
		complete_effect = {
			add_stability = -0.05
		}
		
		remove_effect = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = GER_Desperate_Struggle1_idea
				add_idea = GER_Desperate_Struggle2_idea
			}
		}
	}
	
	GER_increase_struggle_level3 = {

		icon = GFX_decision_dec_GER_landsturm

		allowed = { original_tag = GER }
		
		available = {
			has_war_support > 0
			has_country_flag = GER_Desperate_Struggle_Flag
			has_completed_focus = GER_Landsturm
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = GER_landsturm_is_recruiting
			}
		}
		visible = {
			NOT = { FROM = { has_state_flag = GER_landsturm_recruited_@ROOT } }
			has_defensive_war = yes
		}
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > 499 }
			command_power > 4
		}
		custom_cost_text = 500_infantry_equipment_1_cp_5
		days_remove = 15
		cancel_if_not_visible = yes
		on_map_mode = map_only

		state_target = yes
		target_array = GER.owned_states
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		target_root_trigger = {
			has_country_flag = GER_Desperate_Struggle_Flag
			has_defensive_war = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 200
		}

		cancel_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision cancel GER_recruit_landsturm target: [From.GetName]"
			hidden_effect = {
				add_command_power = 5
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 500
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision GER_recruit_landsturm target: [From.GetName]"
			set_country_flag = GER_landsturm_is_recruiting
			hidden_effect = {
				add_command_power = -5
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = -500
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GER_recruit_landsturm target: [From.GetName]"
			clr_country_flag = GER_landsturm_is_recruiting
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = GER_landsturm_recruited_@ROOT
				### calculate number of units to spawn
				set_temp_variable = { units_to_spawn = state_population_k }
				divide_temp_variable = { units_to_spawn = 500 }

				### failsafe so at least one unit is always spawned
				clamp_temp_variable = { var = units_to_spawn min = 1 max = 7 }

				### tooltip
				set_temp_variable = { total_units_to_spawn = units_to_spawn }
				custom_effect_tooltip = GER_recruit_landsturm_units

				### spawn normal militias
				set_temp_variable = { counter = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = units_to_spawn value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
					ROOT = { 
						if = {
							limit = {
								NOT = { has_template = "Landsturm" }
							}
							division_template = {
								name = "Landsturm"
								division_names_group = GER_MILINF_01
								regiments = {
									irregular_infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
									irregular_infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
									irregular_infantry = { x = 0 y = 2 }
									irregular_infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
									irregular_infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
									irregular_infantry = { x = 1 y = 2 }
									irregular_infantry = { x = 2 y = 0 }
								}						
								support = {
									anti_tank = { x = 0 y = 0 }
								}
								priority = 0
								template_counter = 68
							}
						}
					}
					for_loop_effect = {
						start = 1
						end = units_to_spawn
						compare = less_than_or_equals

						meta_effect = {
							text = {
								create_unit = {
									division = "name = [DIVISION] division_template = \"Landsturm\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 1"
									allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
									owner = ROOT
								}
							}
							DIVISION = "\\\"[?counter]a [THIS.GetName] Landsturm Division\\\""
						}
						add_to_temp_variable = { counter = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

french_occupation_decisions = {

	GER_erode_cof_legitimacy = {
		icon = eng_propaganda_campaigns

		allowed = {
			NOT = { tag = FRA }
		}

		available = {
			FRA = {
				is_government_in_exile = yes
				has_legitimacy > 20
			}
		}

		visible = {
			has_global_flag = FRA_capitulated
			FRA = { is_government_in_exile = yes }
			has_war_with = FRA
			is_subject = no

			set_temp_variable = { cof_states = 0 }
			FRA = {
				all_owned_state = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_core_of = FRA
							is_controlled_by = ROOT
						}
						add_to_temp_variable = { var = ROOT.cof_states value = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			check_variable = { cof_states > 2 }
		}

		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 30
		cost = 25

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1000
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision GER_erode_cof_legitimacy"
			set_country_flag = GER_eroding_cof_leg
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove GER_erode_cof_legitimacy"
			clr_country_flag = GER_eroding_cof_leg
			if = {
				limit = { tag = NFA }
				FRA = {
					add_legitimacy = -25
					add_war_support = -0.15
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					country_exists = NFA
					NOT = { tag = GER }
				}
				FRA = {
					add_legitimacy = -15
					add_war_support = -0.10
				}
			}
			else = {
				FRA = {
					add_legitimacy = -10
					add_war_support = -0.05
				}
			}
		}
	}

	GER_invite_nfa_to_france = {
		icon = generic_agreement

		allowed = { tag = GER }

		available = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = NFA }
		}

		visible = {
			has_war_with = FRA
			has_global_flag = FRA_capitulated
			FRA = { is_government_in_exile = yes }
			FROM = {
				has_war_with = FRA
				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
			}

			set_temp_variable = { cof_states = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { nfa_states = 0 }
			FRA = {
				all_owned_state = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_core_of = FRA
							is_controlled_by = GER
							region = 20
						}
						add_to_temp_variable = { ROOT.cof_states = 1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_core_of = FRA
							is_controlled_by = NFA
						}
						add_to_temp_variable = { ROOT.nfa_states = 1 }
						subtract_from_temp_variable = { ROOT.cof_states = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			check_variable = { cof_states > 0 }
			check_variable = { nfa_states < 3 }
		}

		target_root_trigger = {
			has_war_with = FRA
		}

		targets = { NFA }

		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				has_war_with = FRA
				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes
		cost = 0

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_country_flag = GER_eroding_cof_leg
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_political_power > 50
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FRA = { has_legitimacy < 50 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					NOT = { country_exists = ENG }
					ENG = { surrender_progress > 0.2 }
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision GER_invite_nfa_to_france target: [From.GetName]"
			NFA = { country_event = capt.43 }
		}
	}
}

	GER_desperate_struggle_dec1 = {
		icon = GFX_decision_dec_GER_struggle
		
		allowed = { tag = GER }
		
		activation = {
			OR = {
				has_war_with = FRA
				has_war_with = ENG
				has_war_with = RUS
			}
			has_capitulated = no
			surrender_progress < 0.40
			has_idea = GER_Desperate_Struggle3_idea
			NOT = { has_idea = GER_Desperate_Struggle2_idea }
			has_country_flag = GER_Desperate_Struggle_Flag
		}
		
		available = {
			has_capitulated = yes
		}
		
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				surrender_progress > 0.40
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = FRA
					has_war_with = ENG
					has_war_with = RUS
				}
			}
		}
		
		is_good = yes
		days_mission_timeout = 30

		timeout_effect = {
			log = "[GetLogRoot][GetLogFrom]: Decision timeout GER_desperate_struggle_dec1"
			effect_tooltip = {
				country_event = gerdom.513 # Struggle Lowers from 3 to 2
			}
		}
	}
	
	GER_desperate_struggle_dec2 = {
		icon = GFX_decision_dec_GER_struggle
		
		allowed = { tag = GER }
		
		activation = {
			OR = {
				has_war_with = FRA
				has_war_with = ENG
				has_war_with = RUS
			}
			has_capitulated = no
			surrender_progress < 0.20
			has_idea = GER_Desperate_Struggle2_idea
			NOT = { has_idea = GER_Desperate_Struggle1_idea }
			has_country_flag = GER_Desperate_Struggle_Flag
		}
		
		available = {
			has_capitulated = yes
		}
		
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				surrender_progress > 0.20
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = FRA
					has_war_with = ENG
					has_war_with = RUS
				}
			}
		}
		
		is_good = yes
		days_mission_timeout = 30

		timeout_effect = {
			log = "[GetLogRoot][GetLogFrom]: Decision timeout GER_desperate_struggle_dec2"
			effect_tooltip = {
				country_event = gerdom.514 # Struggle Lowers from 2 to 1
			}
		}
	}
	
	GER_desperate_struggle_dec3 = {
		icon = GFX_decision_dec_GER_struggle
		
		allowed = { tag = GER }
		
		activation = {
			OR = {
				has_war_with = FRA
				has_war_with = ENG
				has_war_with = RUS
			}
			has_capitulated = no
			surrender_progress < 0.05
			has_idea = GER_Desperate_Struggle1_idea
			has_country_flag = GER_Desperate_Struggle_Flag
		}
		
		available = {
			has_capitulated = yes
		}
		
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				surrender_progress > 0.05
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = FRA
					has_war_with = ENG
					has_war_with = RUS
				}
			}
		}
		
		is_good = yes
		days_mission_timeout = 30
		
		timeout_effect = {
			log = "[GetLogRoot][GetLogFrom]: Decision timeout GER_desperate_struggle_dec3"
			effect_tooltip = {
				country_event = gerdom.515 # Clears Struggle
			}
		}
	}
